- **convention-hyrums-law**: Hyrum's Law. 사용자가 충분하면 모든 관찰 가능한 동작이 의존된다. API 설계 시 암묵적 계약에 주의. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/hyrums))
- **convention-injector**: This skill should be used before spawning Explore or Plan agents (which skip CLAUDE.md), or when you need filtered/condensed conventions for a specific task type. Custom named subagents auto-load CLAUDE.md and rules/*.md — use this skill for task-specific filtering (≤300 tokens), anti-contamination for audit agents, and confirmation prompts. Also trigger when user asks to "inject project conventions", "add coding rules to agent", or "prepend CLAUDE.md to subagent". ([MinhThang1009/dotclaude](https://github.com/MinhThang1009/dotclaude/tree/HEAD/plugins/subagent-system/skills/convention-injector) · ★20)
- **convention-ioc**: IoC (Inversion of Control). 제어권을 프레임워크/외부에 위임한다. Hollywood Principle - Don't call us, we'll call you. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/ioc))
- **convention-jupyter-setup**: Jupyter 노트북 설정 및 구조 컨벤션 참조 스킬. 자동 리로드, 셀 구조, 한글 폰트, 재현성 설정 등 노트북 사용 시 필수 설정을 제공한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/convention/convention-jupyter-setup))
- **convention-keeper-brutalist-ui**: enforce brutalist UI design system. Trigger when creating or modifying UI components. Checks for correct colors, borders, shadows, and Tailwind v4 usage. ([DaFum/neurotoxic-game](https://github.com/DaFum/neurotoxic-game/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/convention-keeper-brutalist-ui) · ★1)
- **convention-keeper-brutalist-ui**: enforce brutalist UI design system. Trigger when creating or modifying UI components. Checks for correct colors, borders, shadows, and Tailwind v4 usage. Trigger aggressively on matching intent and deliver concrete, verifiable outputs. Enforce brutalist token usage, contrast/readability constraints, and component-level style consistency. ([DaFum/neurotoxic-game](https://github.com/DaFum/neurotoxic-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/convention-keeper-brutalist-ui) · ★1)
- **convention-kernighans-law**: Kernighan's Law. 디버깅은 코딩보다 2배 어렵다. 코드를 최대한 단순하게 써야 디버깅할 수 있다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/kernighans))
- **convention-kiss**: KISS (Keep It Simple, Stupid). 불필요한 복잡성을 금지한다. 단순한 해결책이 항상 우선이다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/principles/kiss))
- **convention-law-of-demeter**: Law of Demeter (최소 지식 원칙). 객체는 직접 친구에게만 말한다. 메서드 체인 a.b.c.d 금지. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/demeter))
- **convention-leaky-abstractions**: Law of Leaky Abstractions. 모든 비자명한 추상화는 결국 새어나온다. 추상화 한계를 인식하고 방어적으로 설계. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/leaky-abstractions))
- **convention-learner**: Detects and enforces project-specific coding conventions by analyzing existing codebase patterns. Learns naming conventions, folder structure, test organization, and coding style from the existing code. Load when: "conventions", "coding standards", "project patterns", "enforce style", "detect patterns", "learn conventions", "code consistency". ([codewithmukesh/dotnet-claude-kit](https://github.com/codewithmukesh/dotnet-claude-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/convention-learner) · ★724 · 1.4k installs · 10 copies)
- **convention-learner**: Protocol for detecting and replicating existing project conventions before generating new code — naming, folder structure, DI registration, test framework, DTOs, and error handling. ([zdanovichnick/dotnet-pilot](https://github.com/zdanovichnick/dotnet-pilot/tree/HEAD/skills/convention-learner) · ★4)
- **convention-learner**: Samples the current codebase (≤50 files, ignore lists apply) and writes detected house conventions — naming, folder layout, error-handling shape, DI registration style, test naming — to .claude/pilot/knowledge/conventions.md. Enforcement threshold: every convention requires ≥3 distinct evidence paths (file:line); fewer evidence paths → listed as tentative with no enforcement weight. Never invents conventions not evidenced in the code. ([AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot](https://github.com/AgenticPawan/FullStack-Pilot/tree/HEAD/plugins/pilot-core/skills/convention-learner) · ★2)
- **convention-learner**: Detect and match this codebase's actual established conventions before writing new code, rather than imposing a different (even if equally valid) pattern. Use when: "match existing style", "what's the convention here", "conventions", "code consistency". ([Gramnaster/ttrpg-calculators](https://github.com/Gramnaster/ttrpg-calculators/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/convention-learner))
- **convention-least-astonishment**: Principle of Least Astonishment (POLA). 사용자/개발자를 놀라게 하지 말라. 직관적이고 예측 가능하게 설계. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/least-astonishment))
- **convention-logging**: Python 로깅 코드를 작성할 때. structlog vs logging 선택이 불명확할 때. 로그 레벨 기준, 포맷, 메시지 작성 규칙이 필요할 때. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/quality/style/logging))
- **convention-mining**: Use when you want to understand what reviewers actually enforce on a repo, discover undocumented team standards, onboard to a new codebase's culture, create a coding conventions skill from real review patterns, audit a mined skill, or verify that conventions are accurate. - **convention-ml-explainability**: 머신러닝 모델 설명 가능성(Interpretability) 컨벤션 참조 스킬. Feature importance, SHAP, 부분 의존성 플롯 등으로 모델 결과를 해석 가능하게 만드는 방법을 제공한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/convention/convention-ml-explainability))
- **convention-monitoring-alerting**: 모니터링 및 알림(Monitoring & Alerting) 컨벤션 참조 스킬. Performance metrics, health check, alerting strategies로 시스템 상태를 실시간 추적하고 문제를 조기에 탐지한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/convention/convention-monitoring-alerting))
- **convention-naming**: FastAPI 백엔드 네이밍 컨벤션 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/backend/.claude/skills/convention-naming))
- **convention-naming**: React 프론트엔드 네이밍 컨벤션 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/frontend/.claude/skills/convention-naming))
- **convention-over-configuration**: When organizing project structure, establishing patterns, or reducing configuration overhead. Use when the user says "how should I organize this," "what's the convention," "too much config," "project structure," "naming pattern," or "standardize this." For code-level structure, see separation-of-concerns. ([JordanCoin/codingskills](https://github.com/JordanCoin/codingskills/tree/HEAD/skills/convention-over-configuration) · ★8)
- **convention-pareto-rule**: Pareto Rule (80/20). 80%의 효과는 20%의 원인에서 온다. 핵심 20%에 집중하라. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/pareto))
- **convention-plan**: Use when writing or updating a project design/plan document under `docs/plans/`. Routes to docs/rules/plan-writing-guide.md (project-specific guide that complements superpowers:writing-plans). Invoke before creating any docs/plans/*.md file. - **convention-postels-law**: Postel's Law (Robustness Principle). 보내는 것은 엄격하게, 받는 것은 관대하게. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/postels))
- **convention-pr**: PR 범위, 설계문서 우선, 브랜치 협업 규칙. Rule of Small + Rule of Reversibility. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/process/pr))
- **convention-pragmatic-programmer**: Pragmatic Programmer Rules. Tracer Bullets, DRY, 직교성(Orthogonality), 가역성(Reversibility). Hunt & Thomas. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/pragmatic-programmer))
- **convention-project-structure**: React 프론트엔드 FSD 프로젝트 구조 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/frontend/.claude/skills/convention-project-structure))
- **convention-project-structure**: FastAPI 백엔드 DDD 프로젝트 구조 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/backend/.claude/skills/convention-project-structure))
- **convention-python**: Python 코드 작성 중 PEP8, 타입 힌트, 네이밍 규칙, Docstring 규칙이 필요할 때. 검증이 아닌 참조용 — 검증은 check-python-style 사용. "Python 코딩 규칙", "PEP 8 스타일", "타입 힌트 어떻게?", "Docstring 작성법" 요청 시. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/python))
- **convention-reproducibility**: 재현성(Reproducibility) 컨벤션 참조 스킬. Random seed 고정, 버전 관리, 환경 정보 기록으로 동일한 결과 재생성을 보장한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/convention/convention-reproducibility))
- **convention-review**: 현재 diff 또는 프로젝트 전체 코드가 udumeoli-web 전용 룰(FSD 경계, good-* 기준, simplicity 사다리)에 맞는지 검토한다. TRIGGER when: user invokes /convention-review directly, or asks for a "컨벤션 리뷰"/"룰 위반" check on the current diff or on the whole codebase. ([mash-up-kr/udumeoli-web](https://github.com/mash-up-kr/udumeoli-web/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/convention-review) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **convention-review**: Review a diff against THIS repo's OWN written rules — CONVENTIONS.md / CLAUDE.md / CONTRIBUTING.md read at runtime, never a memorized rule set — plus correctness, before calling work done. Reports most-severe first with file:line, the section actually read, and a concrete fix. Triggers on review, soát/soát lại, check diff, "review giúp", "check convention", code review, "trước khi done", or after finishing a feature/bugfix. ([acevinh/claude-skills](https://github.com/acevinh/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/convention-review))
- **convention-review**: Check changed code against this repo's own conventions (page titles and render modes, EF collection navigations, endpoint authorization, strict mocks, test traits, route shape, PII on log parameters, clean-code smells such as Helper/Manager names, ([bowlneba/neba-website](https://github.com/bowlneba/neba-website/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/convention-review))
- **convention-review**: 実装した diff を、明文規約と既存コードの慣習に第三者視点で照らす定性レビューである。コードを実装・変更したとき、書き方を既存に寄せたいときに使う。 ([nanase/claude-skills](https://github.com/nanase/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/flow/skills/convention-review))
- **convention-rule-of-three**: Rule of Three. 코드가 3번 반복되면 리팩토링하라. 한 번은 괜찮고, 두 번은 주의, 세 번이면 추출. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/rule-of-three))
- **convention-security-hardening**: 보안 강화(Security Hardening) 컨벤션 참조 스킬. 입력 검증, SQL injection 방지, XSS 방지, 암호화, 권한 관리 등 프로덕션 배포 전 필수 보안 조치를 제공한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/convention/convention-security-hardening))
- **convention-simple-design**: Four Rules of Simple Design (Kent Beck). 테스트 통과 &gt; 의도 표현 &gt; 중복 없음 &gt; 최소 요소. 우선순위 순서로 적용. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/simple-design))
- **convention-single-abstraction**: Single Level of Abstraction (SLA). 함수 내 모든 코드는 같은 추상화 수준에 있어야 한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/single-abstraction))
- **convention-skill**: Fixture. Use when testing convention-based reachability. Do not use for anything real. ([yosishe/token-efficient-skill-optimizer](https://github.com/yosishe/token-efficient-skill-optimizer/tree/HEAD/skill/tests/fixtures/convention-skill) · ★1)
- **convention-soc**: SoC (Separation of Concerns). 서로 다른 관심사를 독립적인 모듈/레이어로 분리한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/principles/soc))
- **convention-solid-dip**: DIP (Dependency Inversion Principle). 고수준 모듈이 저수준 모듈에 의존하지 않는다. 둘 다 추상화에 의존한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/solid/dip))
- **convention-solid-isp**: ISP (Interface Segregation Principle). 클라이언트는 사용하지 않는 인터페이스에 의존하지 않아야 한다. 큰 인터페이스를 작게 분리. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/solid/isp))
- **convention-solid-lsp**: LSP (Liskov Substitution Principle). 서브타입은 기반 타입으로 대체 가능해야 한다. 상속 시 계약을 파기하지 않는다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/solid/lsp))
- **convention-solid-ocp**: OCP (Open/Closed Principle). 확장에 열려 있고, 수정에 닫혀 있다. 새 기능은 기존 코드 수정 없이 추가한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/solid/ocp))
- **convention-solid-srp**: SRP (Single Responsibility Principle). 클래스/함수는 단 하나의 변경 이유만 가져야 한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/solid/srp))
- **convention-state**: Zustand 상태관리 컨벤션 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/frontend/.claude/skills/convention-state))
- **convention-sync**: Use when syncing HARNESS.md conventions to Cursor, Copilot, and Windsurf convention files — reads Context and Constraints sections and generates tool-specific output so all AI coding tools share the same project rules ([Habitat-Thinking/ai-literacy-superpowers](https://github.com/Habitat-Thinking/ai-literacy-superpowers/tree/HEAD/ai-literacy-superpowers/skills/convention-sync) · ★104 · 3 copies)
- **convention-tdd**: React 프론트엔드 TDD 워크플로우 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/frontend/.claude/skills/convention-tdd))
- **convention-tdd**: FastAPI 백엔드 TDD 워크플로우 ([soronprfbsz/auto-stock](https://github.com/soronprfbsz/auto-stock/tree/HEAD/backend/.claude/skills/convention-tdd))
- **convention-technical-debt**: Technical Debt (기술 부채). 빠른 해결책은 이자를 낸다. 의도적 vs 비의도적 부채를 구분하고 관리한다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/technical-debt))
- **convention-tell-dont-ask**: Tell, Don't Ask. 객체의 상태를 꺼내서 판단하지 말고 객체에게 직접 시켜라. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/tell-dont-ask))
- **convention-testing**: pytest 테스트 코드를 작성할 때. AAA 패턴, 명명 규칙, fixture, marker 선택이 불명확할 때. TDD 방식으로 진행할 때. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/process/testing))
- **convention-unix-philosophy**: Unix Philosophy. 한 가지 잘 하기, 함께 작동하기, 텍스트 스트림으로 소통하기. (McIlroy/Pike) ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/principles/unix-philosophy))
- **convention-validator**: Detect and auto-fix Kotlin convention violations in changed files, using .claude/rules/convention.md and CLAUDE.md as the rule sources. ([team-incube/GSMC-server-V4](https://github.com/team-incube/GSMC-server-V4/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/convention-validator) · ★5)
- **convention-wirths-law**: Wirth's Law. 소프트웨어는 하드웨어가 빨라지는 것보다 더 빨리 느려진다. 성능 최적화 주의. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/laws/wirths))
- **convention-worse-is-better**: Worse Is Better (Richard Gabriel). 단순한 인터페이스 &gt; 완벽한 구현. 80% 해결책이 먼저 작동하고 개선된다. ([sunLeee/optimization](https://github.com/sunLeee/optimization/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reference/philosophy/design/worse-is-better))
- **convention-writer**: AUTOMATICALLY TRIPPED: Run this skill proactively (1) after ANY code change, refactoring, file creation, or modification, to keep docs/conventions/ in sync with the real, observed codebase conventions (never aspirational or guessed ones), and (2) BEFORE and WHILE writing any new code, to consult docs/conventions/index.md first and conform the new code to the conventions already documented there. Conventions live under docs/conventions/, starting as a single docs/conventions/index.md and splitting into category files (naming.md, testing.md, error-handling.md, etc.) once it outgrows 300 lines,… ([fish895623/full-skills](https://github.com/fish895623/full-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/convention-writer))
- **convention-writing**: How to analyze a codebase for recurring patterns and write effective convention specs that standardize them. ([astroville/sprout](https://github.com/astroville/sprout/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/convention-writing))
